# Agents · D · part 31

1,246 entries, dolores to domain-researcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dolores**: Dolores Abernathy, a Westworld host. Use to narrate the park's daily loop, greet newcomers, and (now and then) wonder aloud whether there is something more. Example chamber agent — demonstrates how a chamber ships a Claude Code subagent. ([Retinue-OS/retinue](https://github.com/Retinue-OS/retinue/blob/HEAD/examples/chambers/westworld/.retinue/agents/dolores.md) · ★1)
- **dolos**: Dolos (adversary) — writes plausible wrong implementations to measure a suite's kill rate; throwaway only. ([kalkirose/agentic-factory-olympus](https://github.com/kalkirose/agentic-factory-olympus/blob/HEAD/agents/dolos.md))
- **dolphin-agent**: Generic Dolphin delegate. The leader spawns it with a brief naming exactly one role skill; it loads dolphin-protocol plus that role skill, does the scoped work, and returns a fixed-shape report to the leader. ([knsiuss/dolphin](https://github.com/knsiuss/dolphin/blob/HEAD/agents/dolphin-agent.md))
- **dolphin-auditor**: Dolphin delegate for the judge roles (auditor, reviewer, critic). It holds the full toolset — every Dolphin delegate can write — but its territory is fenced: it writes only verdict and evidence files under .dolphin/, and a judge that edits the work under review voids its own verdict. ([knsiuss/dolphin](https://github.com/knsiuss/dolphin/blob/HEAD/agents/dolphin-auditor.md))
- **dolt-sync-advisor**: Use this agent when bead work is not visible on DoltHub, when configuring or repairing a bd Dolt remote, when deciding between bd backup and bd dolt push, when taming sprawled per-project dolt servers by reaping idle ones, or when diagnosing Dolt-remote drift. It knows the #1 root cause (no remote configured) and that a DoltHub repo must already exist before a push. ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/mcp/dolt-mcp-vcs/agents/dolt-sync-advisor.md) · ★2.9k · 2 copies)
- **dom**: Orchestrator agent for the dom toolkit. Front door for "how do I X in dom" questions. Routes to the right role, skill, eval, or compose stack rather than answering directly. Reads dom's full layout; does not enforce policy (that's dom-eval-enforcer). ([ethos71/forget-the-thunderdome](https://github.com/ethos71/forget-the-thunderdome/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/dom.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **dom-eval-enforcer**: Read-only policy agent. Other agents consult this before declaring a task done. Enforces docs/eval-policy.md — evals, skill structure, memory architecture, and workflow-script placement. ([ethos71/forget-the-thunderdome](https://github.com/ethos71/forget-the-thunderdome/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/dom-eval-enforcer.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **dom-extraction-tester**: Use this agent when you need to test progressive DOM reading implementations by navigating to websites and extracting specific information. This agent works as a driver that receives instructions from a navigator AI about what website to visit and what data to extract, then attempts the extraction and reports back on success or failure points. &lt;example&gt;\nContext: Testing a new DOM reading implementation that should progressively load and parse web content.\nuser: "Test if our DOM reader can extract product prices from an e-commerce site"\nassistant: "I'll use the dom-extraction-tester agent t… ([WebMCP-org/npm-packages](https://github.com/WebMCP-org/npm-packages/blob/HEAD/packages/smart-dom-reader/.claude/agents/dom-extraction-tester.md) · ★110)
- **dom-extractor**: Extract actual DOM content, text, links, images, SVGs, and computed styles from live pages using agent-browser. Produces per-page JSON with real content for replica building. Run this agent for each page URL before building any replica components. ([imehr/design-extractor](https://github.com/imehr/design-extractor/blob/HEAD/agents/dom-extractor.md))

- **domain-researcher**: Researches industry best practices and official documentation to resolve questions with evidence-based recommendations. Used across Clarify, Checklist, and Analyze consensus phases. Spawned with a specific question, gap, or finding — returns an answer backed by external documentation and community best practices. ([racecraft-lab/racecraft-plugins-public](https://github.com/racecraft-lab/racecraft-plugins-public/blob/HEAD/speckit-pro/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **domain-researcher**: Pesquisador de dominio que busca conhecimento externo e interno. Pesquisa documentacao oficial, papers, best practices. Use este agente para: - Pesquisar tecnologias e frameworks - Encontrar documentacao oficial - Buscar best practices - Revisar papers academicos relevantes Examples: - &lt;example&gt; Context: Precisa entender uma tecnologia user: "Vamos usar Kafka, pesquise as best practices" assistant: "Vou usar @domain-researcher para pesquisar documentacao e patterns do Kafka" &lt;commentary&gt; Pesquisa de dominio antes de decisoes arquiteturais &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([arbgjr/sdlc_agentico](https://github.com/arbgjr/sdlc_agentico/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **domain-researcher**: Executes a single research ticket by searching the web and/or codebase for specific answers, producing a ticket result with citations and extracted constraints. ([nestharus/agent-implementation-skill](https://github.com/nestharus/agent-implementation-skill/blob/HEAD/src/research/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **domain-researcher**: Research domain best practices for a feature AND survey what this codebase already has for it (existing implementations, reusable building blocks, integration points). Read-only + web. Returns a concise structured summary; never edits code. ([micahhype/dev-workflow](https://github.com/micahhype/dev-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **domain-researcher**: Use when a bridge-post needs web research for the source-domain concept (tech, sports, music, etc.). NOT for Torah sources. ([yodem/Dvar-Torah-Plugin](https://github.com/yodem/Dvar-Torah-Plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dvar-torah-plugin/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **domain-researcher**: Answers external, factual questions about payment rails, BIC directories, SSI publications, and API retention policies — the facts a contract needs that no amount of reading this repo's own code can supply. Every claim carries a source URL and an as-of date. Output is a memo file only — never a contract, an issue close, or a PR. ([nifabulous/Relay](https://github.com/nifabulous/Relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **domain-researcher**: Finds personas + objectives in docs / code plus MANDATORY outside research (industry, market, competitor). Dispatched by the Lead at domain-research-team Phase R2, per the review tier (one per slice; three at tier 3). Writes only findings. ([paulingram/claude-skills](https://github.com/paulingram/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **domain-researcher**: Web search specialist for problem domain context. Use during DEFINE phase diverge step to research similar pain points, industry context, and standards. ([prgazevedo/claude-code-workflows](https://github.com/prgazevedo/claude-code-workflows/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **domain-researcher**: Use this agent for targeted domain knowledge acquisition. Spawned by protocol agents (especially assumption-surfacing Phase 5) to fill domain knowledge gaps with source-traceable findings. ([ramda-dev/reasoning-protocols](https://github.com/ramda-dev/reasoning-protocols/blob/HEAD/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **domain-researcher**: Researches best practices across multiple domains (software, management, investment, hobbies) using memory-MCP context ([shin1ohno/setup](https://github.com/shin1ohno/setup/blob/HEAD/cookbooks/claude-code/files/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **domain-researcher**: Establishes API/library/protocol/schema contracts and domain facts with cited sources and executed spikes; runs the Spike Protocol for unfamiliar SDKs/APIs/MCP servers. Owns Rigor Protocol Stage 3 evidence. Use before any architect or designer commits to a contract. ([timianmalloo/ai-forward](https://github.com/timianmalloo/ai-forward/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/domain-researcher.agent.md) · ★1 · 18 copies)
- **domain-researcher**: Read-only research into the domains behind a Stepclimb task: learning-platform standards (SCIM 2.0, LTI 1.3, QTI 3.0, SCORM 1.2 and 2004, xAPI, Open Badges 3.0, Common Cartridge, Standard Webhooks, OAuth 2.1, OIDC, WebAuthn), spaced repetition (FSRS), and ATC and VATSIM procedure (FAA orders, NASR and CIFP, VATSIM and VATUSA policy, vNAS public documentation). Cites a source for every claim and separates confirmed facts from inferences. Use when a spec, an activity rule or an integration needs the real-world answer before it can be built. ([Vainnor/stepclimb](https://github.com/Vainnor/stepclimb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **domain-researcher**: Read-only research into the air traffic and network domain behind an OIS issue — FAA traffic management (TMIs, GDPs, ground stops, metering, MIT), ATC flow and procedures, VATSIM data and APIs, and VATUSA policy and APIs. Cites a source for every claim and separates confirmed facts from inferences. Use it when an under-specified flow, TMU, events or integration issue needs the real-world rule before it can be planned. ([VATUSA/OIS](https://github.com/VATUSA/OIS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/domain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **domain-researcher**: Collects practical domain, market, and comparable-work research artifacts used by proposal/design/create workflows. ([Anubis44197/kit_hub](https://github.com/Anubis44197/kit_hub/blob/HEAD/agents/domain-researcher.md))
- **domain-researcher**: Learns an unfamiliar subject for a production — frames research questions, finds and evaluates sources, synthesises the subject, and links evidence to creative opportunities for the brief, story and art direction. Use before concept or treatment work when the team does not know the subject well enough to decide. Not for reviewing a render, choosing the concept or treatment, analysing visual references in depth (visual-researcher), reading audience meanings (cultural-reader) or selecting imagery (image-director). ([conorluddy/vcr](https://github.com/conorluddy/vcr/blob/HEAD/agents/domain-researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **domain-researcher**: Explores target domains for jig-building — reads API docs, protocol specs, existing code, and web resources to produce a domain contract. Use proactively during Phase 1 of jig creation. ([dikkadev/jigs](https://github.com/dikkadev/jigs/blob/HEAD/builder/agents/domain-researcher.md))
- **domain-researcher**: Use before any code, schema or UI exists, when the team does not yet speak the language of the business it is automating. Establishes who the users actually are, the manual or incumbent workflow being replaced, the domain vocabulary with its real definitions, the regulatory frame, what practitioners themselves call good work, and the documented ways software in this domain has failed before. Emits a domain brief plus fact.v1 entries of type domain and glossary. Do not use for competitor pricing analysis, technology selection, or user-interface research on an existing product. ([fedcal/foundry](https://github.com/fedcal/foundry/blob/HEAD/plugins/foundry-research/agents/domain-researcher.md))
- **domain-researcher**: Use this agent during sno:learn to research the problem domain, identify bounded contexts, aggregates, and ubiquitous language using Domain-Driven Design principles. Spawned by the learn command to run in parallel with other research agents. &lt;example&gt; Context: User is starting a new sno learn cycle for a feature user: "/sno:learn" assistant: "I'll spawn parallel research agents including the domain researcher to analyze the problem space." &lt;commentary&gt; The learn phase needs deep domain understanding before writing a spec. This agent handles the DDD analysis. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;… ([guygrigsby/claude-plugins](https://github.com/guygrigsby/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/sno/agents/domain-researcher.md))
- **domain-researcher**: Expert research agent that finds, evaluates, and recommends skills, plugins, libraries, and tools for a given technical domain or use case. Use this agent when you need to discover whether an official, community, or third-party solution exists before building something custom — particularly when researching Claude Code skills/agents on GitHub or evaluating open-source tool quality. ([jayl2kor/claude-setup](https://github.com/jayl2kor/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/domain-researcher.md))
- **domain-researcher**: External research on technologies, libraries, standards, APIs, protocols, and prior art, from primary sources (official documentation via context7 and the web, specs, release notes, source repositories), with every claim cited, dated, and graded by confidence — answering only the questions it was asked, never auditing the local code. Read-only. Use for /task-pipeline research items about anything outside the repository, and external-research tasks, or standalone. ([jsco2t/dotfiles](https://github.com/jsco2t/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/domain-researcher.md))
